Division No. 8, District Solapur hereby, delineate
area of operation of following WUA’s and direct
that the certified copy of the updated map and list
1 2 3 4
of landholders and /or occupiers of said WUA’s
shall be displayed on the notice board of the
sub-division and Division Office and Division and
other prominent public places.
2 318/2 2.40
I, Dhananjay Jivandhar Kondekar, Executive
3 318/3 0.81
Engineer, also hereby, declare that no water shall
4 321 2.67
be supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an
5 321/1 2.02 individual holder or occupiers of such land and the
system of supply of water through Water User
6 322/1/† 1.17
Associations shall by binding on all the holders and
the occupiers of the lands under Maharashtra
8 322/2 2.02 Management Irrigation System by Farmers Act,
9 359 3.53 2005.
Any person affected by this Notification or part
thereof, may file an appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Solapur Irrigation Project Circle within 30
days from the date of publication of this Notification
in the Maharashtra Government Gazette.

1 2 3 4
been decided to delineate the area of Operation
of Water User Associations (WUA’s) on Volumetric
under Section 5.0, 6.0, 7.0 and Rule 3 of the
Maharashtra Mangement of Irrigation Systems by
Farmers Act, 2005. I, Dhananjay Jivandhar
1 94/1 0.90 Kondekar, Executive Engineer, Ujjani Canal
Division No. 8, District Solapur hereby, delineate
2 94/2 0.89
area of operation of following WUA’s and direct
3 94/3 0.89 that the certified copy of the updated map and list
of landholders and /or occupiers of said WUA’s
4 95/1 0.82
shall be displayed on the notice board of the
5 95/2 1.66
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section,
6 98/1 0.91 sub-division and Division Office and Division and
other prominent public places.
7 98/2 0.90
I, Dhananjay Jivandhar Kondekar, Executive
8 99 0.87
Engineer, also hereby, declare that no water shall
9 100/1 1.24
be supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an
individual holder or occupiers of such land and the 10 100/2 1.24
system of supply of water through Water User
11 101 0.05
Associations shall by binding on all the holders and
12 102/1 0.98 the occupiers of the lands under Maharashtra
Management Irrigation System by Farmers Act
13 102/2 1.00
2005.
14 102/3/† 1.01
Any person affected by this Notification or part
thereof, may file an appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Solapur Irrigation Project Circle within 30
days from the date of publication of this Notification
in the Maharashtra Government Gazette.
